#### Synthesis Summary
11 material issue(s) require attention. Highest-priority issue: issue-001 (high) — Issue-001 is a material governed rating-event authority gap: the ontology cannot provide one shared authoritative rating value when engine grades, report-side manual adjustments, mapping changes, and historical assignments are modeled as mutable/current grade attributes instead of distinct governed events. Controlled deliberation did not leave a blocking unresolved disagreement for the highest-priority issue.

#### Material Issues
- issue-001 (high)
  - affected purpose: Shared, reproducible credit-rating concept authority for the risk engine and reporting system, including past reporting-date replay and auditability. Source finding context: 리스크 엔진과 보고 시스템이 공유할 여신 리스크 개념 권위 기준 제공. Source finding context: 리스크 엔진 산출과 보고 시스템 사용 값 사이의 통제 가능하고 감사 가능한 공유 기준 Source finding context: 리스크 엔진과 보고 시스템이 공유할 신용등급 개념 기준 및 과거 기준일 보고 재현성
  - failure condition: A report team manually adjusts Exposure.risk_grade, a rating mapping changes, or a past reporting date must be reconstructed after grade or override activity occurred. Source finding context: 보고서 팀이 시장 상황을 이유로 Exposure.risk_grade를 수동 조정했지만 리스크 엔진은 RiskRating.grade 기반 원 등급을 쓰거나, 조정의 권위/시점/우선순위를 다르게 해석하는 경우. Source finding context: 보고서 팀이 Exposure.risk_grade를 수동 조정하거나 리스크팀 매핑 테이블 변경으로 소비 시스템별 등급 변환 결과가 달라지는 경우 Source finding context: 등급 스케일 또는 매핑이 변경되거나 보고서 팀이 시장 상황에 따라 Exposure.risk_grade를 수동 조정한 뒤 과거 기준일의 등급을 재현해야 하는 경우
  - impact: Engine outputs, reporting grades, and historical reconstructions can each appear valid while lacking a common authority trail, weakening operational consistency and audit trust. Source finding context: 동일 익스포저에 대해 엔진과 보고서가 서로 다른 리스크 등급을 권위 있는 값으로 간주할 수 있어 공유 기준의 신뢰성과 운영 의사결정의 일관성이 약화된다. Source finding context: 수동 조정의 행위자·시각·근거가 개념화되지 않아 보고 등급 변경의 정당성, 승인 여부, 원천 등급 대비 차이를 검증할 수 없고 운영 리스크와 감사 대응 결함이 발생한다. Source finding context: 공유 권위 문서가 현재 값만 표현하므로 엔진 산출 등급, 차주 내부등급, 보고용 익스포저 등급 중 어느 값이 특정 시점의 권위 값인지 판별할 수 없고, 과거 보고·감사·리스크 재계산의 신뢰성이 손상된다.

- issue-005 (high)
  - affected purpose: The ontology's declared purpose as a shared concept authority for risk engine and reporting systems.
  - failure condition: When a rating scale changes, a new grade is introduced, or report-side manual adjustment policy changes, consumers must independently update conversion logic and may produce inconsistent or non-replayable classifications.
  - impact: This weakens review trust in the ontology as an extensible shared basis: the central classification concept is not change-tolerant, and engine/report continuity depends on undocumented external implementations.
  - evidence: `.onto/review/20260716-acee24d9/round1/evolution.findings.yaml#evolution-candidate-001`, `Embedded Materialized Input: header comment says the model targets shared concept authority for risk engine and report systems.`, `Embedded Materialized Input: `RiskRating.grade` values `[R1, R2, R3, R4, R5]` with note pointing to a risk-team wiki mapping.`, `Embedded Materialized Input: `Exposure.risk_grade` values `[AAA, AA, A, BBB, BB, B, CCC]`.`, `Embedded Materialized Input: `Borrower.internal_rating` note says `1~10 숫자 등급 체계`.`, `Embedded Materialized Input: notes say transformations are performed by each consuming system.`, `Embedded Materialized Input: `classification_rules` states `Exposure.risk_grade는 RiskRating.grade에서 도출하되, 보고서 팀이 시장 상황에 따라 수동 조정할 수 있다.`; `notes` states `차주 등급(internal_rating 1~10, RiskRating R1~R5, Exposure risk_grade AAA~CCC)의 3개 스케일은 역사적 이유로 병존한다. 변환은 각 소비 시스템이 수행한다.``
  - source lenses: evolution
  - action candidates: fix_before_release, fix_now
  - problem definition: Rating conversion among coexisting scales is delegated to external consumer systems instead of modeled as a versioned owned concept, making rating-scale evolution, replay, and engine/report continuity brittle.
  - problem framing: root_cause / next_step_blocker / fix_now / must_close_in_target / observed

- issue-008 (high)
  - affected purpose: Shared concept authority for exposure and collateral management across risk-engine and reporting systems. Source finding context: A change-tolerant concept basis for exposure and collateral management shared across systems. Source finding context: Shared credit-risk concept authority for risk engine and reporting systems. Source finding context: A shared concept authority for risk engines and reporting systems covering exposure and collateral concepts.
  - failure condition: A consumer interprets is_a as inheritance/subtyping, or collateral coverage expands to pooled collateral, multiple valuations, lien priority, guarantees, substitutions, or other collateral-specific lifecycles. Source finding context: When collateral coverage expands beyond one asset directly attached to one exposure, the inherited exposure structure forces collateral records to carry exposure fields or requires a breaking remodel. Source finding context: When consumers interpret `is_a` as inheritance/subtyping, collateral assets become valid Exposure instances while also serving as assets securing exposures. Source finding context: Any consumer that interprets `is_a` semantically will treat collateral assets as credit exposures despite the target also defining them as securing assets.
  - impact: Consumers may apply exposure fields, aggregation, or reporting logic to collateral records, corrupting the exposure/collateral boundary and forcing disruptive remodels for collateral evolution. Source finding context: The current inheritance choice narrows future collateral extensions and increases the likelihood of a disruptive schema change in a core risk-management concept. Source finding context: This can make type-based consumers apply exposure fields, aggregation, or reporting logic to collateral records, undermining the declared shared taxonomy contract. Source finding context: This can corrupt classification, aggregation, and reporting semantics at the boundary between exposure records and collateral records.

- issue-007 (medium)
  - affected purpose: Extensible shared classification ontology for credit-risk engine and reporting concepts.
  - failure condition: A new borrower segment, product type, collateral class, or grade must be introduced while preserving historical meaning and downstream compatibility. Source finding context: When a new segment, product, collateral class, or grade is introduced, the ontology must edit existing enum declarations and downstream validators instead of adding a new taxonomy record with compatibility metadata.
  - impact: Schema edits and consumer validator changes become necessary for ordinary category growth, increasing migration pressure and weakening continuity of the shared classification surface. Source finding context: This creates brittle evolution and migration risk in the core classification surface, especially for systems that rely on the ontology as a shared validation and reporting basis.

  - action candidates: follow_up
  - problem definition: Volatile business classifications are encoded as closed attribute-local enums rather than managed taxonomy concepts with lifecycle and compatibility metadata.

- issue-009 (medium)
  - affected purpose: Shared semantic basis for risk-engine and reporting use of collateralized exposure risk measures.
  - failure condition: Exposure.amount or Collateral.appraised_value changes while the independently entered ltv value remains different, or a consumer needs to decide whether to trust calculated or entered LTV.   - impact: Risk rules and reports can use inconsistent LTV semantics for the same exposure, reducing trust in classification and limit-monitoring outputs. Source finding context: Risk rules and reports can use inconsistent LTV semantics for the same exposure, reducing trust in classification and limit monitoring outputs.
  - evidence: `.onto/review/20260716-acee24d9/round1/semantics.findings.yaml#semantics-candidate-002`, `materialized-input.md:24-37`, `materialized-input.md:39-46`, `.onto/review/20260716-acee24d9/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:24-37,39-46`
  - source lenses: semantics
  - action candidates: fix_before_release, fix_now
  - problem definition: LTV is named as a collateralized ratio but modeled as an independent entered value rather than a governed derived, snapshot, entered, or approved override measure with formula, timing, valuation source, authority, and override semantics.
  - problem framing: root_cause / next_step_blocker / fix_now / must_close_in_target / observed

- issue-010 (medium)
  - affected purpose: A concept authority shared by the risk engine and reporting system for credit-grade classification.
  - failure condition: A consumer must interpret or convert rating scales locally because the ontology does not establish one canonical semantic mapping or distinguish adjusted report grades from engine grades.
  - impact: Risk engine outputs and reporting classifications can disagree while both appear valid under the ontology, weakening the ontology as the shared concept standard.
  - evidence: `.onto/review/20260716-acee24d9/round1/semantics.findings.yaml#semantics-candidate-003`, `materialized-input.md:20-22`, `materialized-input.md:31-34`, `materialized-input.md:48-57`, `materialized-input.md:92-99`, `.onto/review/20260716-acee24d9/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:20-22,31-34,48-57,92-99`
  - source lenses: semantics
  - action candidates: fix_before_release, fix_now
  - problem definition: Historically separate borrower, engine, and reporting grade scales are presented as credit/risk grades without canonical semantic authority, distinct concept names, or explicit mapping inside the shared artifact.
  - problem framing: root_cause / next_step_blocker / fix_now / must_close_in_target / observed

- issue-011 (medium)
  - affected purpose: Shared concept authority for limit/appetite compliance across engine and reporting outputs. Source finding context: The ontology's stated purpose as a shared concept authority for risk engine and reporting systems.
  - failure condition: A consumer needs to compute, audit, or report compliance from ExposureAggregate values, or treats RiskAppetite.compliance_status as intrinsic to the threshold rather than an evaluation result for a specific subject and time. Source finding context: A consumer treats `RiskAppetite.compliance_status` as intrinsic to the appetite threshold rather than as an evaluation result for a specific exposure aggregate and time. Source finding context: When a consumer needs to compute, audit, or report RiskAppetite compliance from ExposureAggregate values, the target threshold/status concept is not reachable from the exposure aggregate graph.
  - impact: Compliance status becomes ambiguous and disconnected, so consumers may invent separate joins or scoping assumptions and produce inconsistent breach outputs. Source finding context: Reports or engine checks can lose the subject and time basis of a breach, making compliance status ambiguous and hard to reconcile. Source finding context: This weakens review trust because a central compliance concept is present but disconnected; different consumers may invent separate joins or scoping assumptions, producing inconsistent compliance outputs from the same ontology.
  - evidence: `.onto/review/20260716-acee24d9/round1/semantics.findings.yaml#semantics-candidate-004`, `materialized-input.md:69-83`, `materialized-input.md:92-94`, `.onto/review/20260716-acee24d9/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:69-83,92-94`, `.onto/review/20260716-acee24d9/round1/structure.findings.yaml#structure-candidate-001`, `.onto/review/20260716-acee24d9/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:7`, `.onto/review/20260716-acee24d9/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:78`, `.onto/review/20260716-acee24d9/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:85`, `.onto/review/20260716-acee24d9/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:78 and .onto/review/20260716-acee24d9/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:85`
  - source lenses: semantics, structure
  - action candidates: fix_before_release, fix_now
  - problem definition: RiskAppetite compliance is modeled as an intrinsic status on a policy threshold and omitted from the navigable relation graph instead of as a scoped evaluation result over an ExposureAggregate at a time.
  - problem framing: root_cause / next_step_blocker / fix_now / must_close_in_target / observed

#### Synthesized Material Issue Explanations
- issue-001 (high): Issue-001 is a material governed rating-event authority gap: the ontology cannot provide one shared authoritative rating value when engine grades, report-side manual adjustments, mapping changes, and historical assignments are modeled as mutable/current grade attributes instead of distinct governed events.
  - issue stance agreement: 4/6
  - agreed or narrowed lenses: coverage, evolution, semantics, axiology
  - issue stance disagreement: 0/6
  - disagreeing stance lenses: none
  - not applicable lenses: structure
  - insufficient evidence lenses: logic
  - resolution accepted by: 5/5 deliberation participants
  - accepted lenses: axiology, coverage, evolution, semantics, logic
  - remaining disagreement: 0/5 deliberation participants
  - remaining disagreement lenses: none
  - raised by lenses: axiology, coverage
  - issue statement: The ontology cannot establish a shared authoritative rating value when report-side manual adjustments, historical rating assignments, and mapping changes are not represented as distinct governed events. Source finding context: credit-risk-ontology.yaml classification_rules / Exposure.risk_grade Source finding context: .onto/review/20260716-acee24d9/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:6-8,31-34,48-56,92-93; .onto/review/20260716-acee24d9/execution-preparation/review-value-alignment-criteria.yaml:5-8 Source finding context: 보고서 팀의 수동 risk_grade 조정 권한이 공유 개념 권위 목적과 어긋난다. Source finding context: 공유 권위 문서라면 엔진 산출 등급과 보고 등급이 언제, 왜, 누구 권한으로 달라지는지 동일하게 해석되어야 한다. 현재 규칙은 보고서 팀의 국소적 필요를 권위 체계 위에 올려놓아 리스크 엔진과 보고 시스템이 서로 다른 등급을 정당한 값으로 사용할 수 있게 한다. 이는 사용자가 요청한 '공유할 개념 기준'과 권위 관점 검토 목적을 직접 약화한다. Source finding context: 수동 조정을 원본 risk_grade 값에 덮어쓰기 가능한 규칙으로 두지 말고, 별도 Override/Adjustment 개념으로 분리해 owner, approval authority, reason, source_grade, adjusted_grade, effective_at/as_of, expiry, precedence, audit_id를 정의하라. 엔진과 보고 시스템 모두 동일한 우선순위 규칙으로 원본 등급과 조정 등급을 해석하게 해야 한다. Source finding context: .onto/review/20260716-acee24d9/round1/axiology.findings.yaml#axiology-candidate-001 Source finding context: 리스크 엔진과 보고 시스템이 공유할 여신 리스크 개념 권위 기준 제공. Source finding context: 보고서 팀이 시장 상황을 이유로 Exposure.risk_grade를 수동 조정했지만 리스크 엔진은 RiskRating.grade 기반 원 등급을 쓰거나, 조정의 권위/시점/우선순위를 다르게 해석하는 경우. Source finding context: 동일 익스포저에 대해 엔진과 보고서가 서로 다른 리스크 등급을 권위 있는 값으로 간주할 수 있어 공유 기준의 신뢰성과 운영 의사결정의 일관성이 약화된다. Source finding context: 등급 조정이라는 별도 권위 행위를 원본 등급 개념 안에 무조건 허용하면서 조정 권위와 우선순위를 모델링하지 않았다. Source finding context: Exposure.risk_grade는 RiskRating.grade에서 도출되지만 보고서 팀이 시장 상황에 따라 수동 조정할 수 있다. Source finding context: RiskRating.grade는 리스크 엔진 산출 등급이고 Exposure.risk_grade는 보고서 팀이 사용하는 등급으로 서로 다른 소비자 권위가 걸려 있다. Source finding context: 대상 문서는 리스크 엔진과 보고서 시스템이 공유하는 개념 권위 문서를 지향한다. Source finding context: credit-risk-ontology.yaml / classification_rules[0] and notes[0] Source finding context: Embedded materialized input: classification_rules[0]; notes[0]; RiskRating.grade note Source finding context: 등급 변환과 보고용 수동 조정에 대한 감사 증거 개념이 누락되어 통제 행위의 행위자·시각·근거를 추적할 수 없다. Source finding context: 수동 조정은 통제가 필요한 행위이며, 리스크 엔진 산출값과 보고 시스템 표시값을 달라지게 할 수 있다. 공유 개념 기준 문서가 조정의 감사 증거 범주를 포함하지 않으면 보고 값이 엔진 값과 다른 이유를 사후에 설명하거나 승인된 조정과 임의 변경을 구분하기 어렵다. Source finding context: ManualRatingAdjustment 또는 RatingOverride 엔티티를 추가하고 original_grade, adjusted_grade, actor, approver, adjusted_at, reason_code, evidence_ref, approval_status, effective_period를 포함한다. 등급 매핑도 MappingVersion/MappingApproval로 모델링해 위키 참조가 아니라 온톨로지 내 권위 개념으로 편입한다. Source finding context: .onto/review/20260716-acee24d9/round1/coverage.findings.yaml#coverage-candidate-002 Source finding context: 리스크 엔진 산출과 보고 시스템 사용 값 사이의 통제 가능하고 감사 가능한 공유 기준 Source finding context: 보고서 팀이 Exposure.risk_grade를 수동 조정하거나 리스크팀 매핑 테이블 변경으로 소비 시스템별 등급 변환 결과가 달라지는 경우 Source finding context: 수동 조정의 행위자·시각·근거가 개념화되지 않아 보고 등급 변경의 정당성, 승인 여부, 원천 등급 대비 차이를 검증할 수 없고 운영 리스크와 감사 대응 결함이 발생한다. Source finding context: 통제 행위인 등급 override와 매핑 변경을 독립된 감사 가능한 사건 개념으로 포함하지 않은 범위 결손 Source finding context: 온톨로지는 보고서 팀의 시장 상황 기반 수동 등급 조정을 허용한다. Source finding context: 등급 변환은 각 소비 시스템이 수행하고 매핑 테이블은 대상 온톨로지 밖의 리스크팀 위키를 참조한다. Source finding context: 대상 엔티티에는 수동 조정 이벤트나 매핑 변경 승인·증거를 담는 개념이 없다. Source finding context: 그 결과 엔진 등급과 보고 등급이 달라지는 행위의 감사 추적 범위가 비어 있다. Source finding context: credit-risk-ontology.yaml / RiskRating, Borrower.internal_rating, Exposure.risk_grade Source finding context: Embedded materialized input: Borrower.attributes.internal_rating note; RiskRating.attributes.grade note; Exposure.attributes.risk_grade note; notes[0]; classification_rules[0] Source finding context: 병존하는 3개 신용등급 스케일에 대해 유효기간, 이력, 매핑 기준시점 개념이 누락되어 과거 시점의 등급 재구성이 불가능하다. Source finding context: 대상 문서는 리스크 엔진과 보고 시스템이 공유하는 개념 권위 문서를 지향한다고 선언한다. 그러나 보고서 팀이 Exposure.risk_grade를 사용하고 수동 조정할 수 있으며, 3개 등급 스케일 변환을 각 소비 시스템이 수행한다고 되어 있어 등급 값은 시스템·시점 의존적이다. 이 상태에서 유효기간과 이력 개념이 없으면 특정 보고 기준일이나 과거 의사결정 시점에 어떤 등급과 매핑이 권위 있었는지 복원할 수 없어 엔진 산출과 보고 값의 공유 기준이 약해진다. Source finding context: 등급 관련 개념을 RatingScale, RatingAssignment, RatingMappingVersion 같은 별도 개념으로 분리하고, 각 등급 부여 및 매핑에 effective_from/effective_to, calculated_at 또는 assigned_at, source_system, superseded_by를 추가한다. Exposure.risk_grade의 수동 조정도 원등급과 조정등급을 구분해 기준시점별로 보존한다. Source finding context: .onto/review/20260716-acee24d9/round1/coverage.findings.yaml#coverage-candidate-001 Source finding context: 리스크 엔진과 보고 시스템이 공유할 신용등급 개념 기준 및 과거 기준일 보고 재현성 Source finding context: 등급 스케일 또는 매핑이 변경되거나 보고서 팀이 시장 상황에 따라 Exposure.risk_grade를 수동 조정한 뒤 과거 기준일의 등급을 재현해야 하는 경우 Source finding context: 공유 권위 문서가 현재 값만 표현하므로 엔진 산출 등급, 차주 내부등급, 보고용 익스포저 등급 중 어느 값이 특정 시점의 권위 값인지 판별할 수 없고, 과거 보고·감사·리스크 재계산의 신뢰성이 손상된다. Source finding context: 등급을 시점 의존 assignment와 versioned mapping으로 모델링하지 않고 여러 엔티티의 현재 속성으로만 둔 개념 범위 결손 Source finding context: 대상 온톨로지는 세 개의 신용등급 스케일을 병존시키고 각 소비 시스템이 변환한다고 설명한다. Source finding context: Exposure.risk_grade는 RiskRating.grade에서 도출되지만 보고서 팀의 수동 조정도 허용한다. Source finding context: 등급 속성에는 적용 기간, 산출/부여 시각, 매핑 버전, 변경 이력 개념이 없다. Source finding context: 따라서 특정 기준일의 권위 등급과 매핑을 재구성하는 데 필요한 시간성 범위가 온톨로지에 포함되지 않는다.
  - affected purpose: Shared, reproducible credit-rating concept authority for the risk engine and reporting system, including past reporting-date replay and auditability. Source finding context: 리스크 엔진과 보고 시스템이 공유할 여신 리스크 개념 권위 기준 제공. Source finding context: 리스크 엔진 산출과 보고 시스템 사용 값 사이의 통제 가능하고 감사 가능한 공유 기준 Source finding context: 리스크 엔진과 보고 시스템이 공유할 신용등급 개념 기준 및 과거 기준일 보고 재현성
  - failure condition: A report team manually adjusts Exposure.risk_grade, a rating mapping changes, or a past reporting date must be reconstructed after grade or override activity occurred. Source finding context: 보고서 팀이 시장 상황을 이유로 Exposure.risk_grade를 수동 조정했지만 리스크 엔진은 RiskRating.grade 기반 원 등급을 쓰거나, 조정의 권위/시점/우선순위를 다르게 해석하는 경우. Source finding context: 보고서 팀이 Exposure.risk_grade를 수동 조정하거나 리스크팀 매핑 테이블 변경으로 소비 시스템별 등급 변환 결과가 달라지는 경우 Source finding context: 등급 스케일 또는 매핑이 변경되거나 보고서 팀이 시장 상황에 따라 Exposure.risk_grade를 수동 조정한 뒤 과거 기준일의 등급을 재현해야 하는 경우
  - impact: Engine outputs, reporting grades, and historical reconstructions can each appear valid while lacking a common authority trail, weakening operational consistency and audit trust. Source finding context: 동일 익스포저에 대해 엔진과 보고서가 서로 다른 리스크 등급을 권위 있는 값으로 간주할 수 있어 공유 기준의 신뢰성과 운영 의사결정의 일관성이 약화된다. Source finding context: 수동 조정의 행위자·시각·근거가 개념화되지 않아 보고 등급 변경의 정당성, 승인 여부, 원천 등급 대비 차이를 검증할 수 없고 운영 리스크와 감사 대응 결함이 발생한다. Source finding context: 공유 권위 문서가 현재 값만 표현하므로 엔진 산출 등급, 차주 내부등급, 보고용 익스포저 등급 중 어느 값이 특정 시점의 권위 값인지 판별할 수 없고, 과거 보고·감사·리스크 재계산의 신뢰성이 손상된다.
  - root hypothesis: Rating changes and report-side overrides are modeled as mutable/current grade attributes rather than time-bound, governed, auditable rating events with authority, effective period, approval, and replay metadata.

  - source lenses: axiology, coverage
  - action candidates: fix_before_release, fix_now
  - materiality: This weakens the declared purpose because the artifact is meant to give the risk engine and reporting system a shared, reproducible credit-rating concept authority. If Exposure.risk_grade can be manually adjusted for reporting, mappings can change outside the ontology, and past ratings lack effective-period and audit metadata, then engine output, reporting display, and historical reconstruction can each look valid while pointing to different authoritative values.
  - root cause: The root cause is the under-modeling of rating changes, mappings, assignments, and report-side overrides as current grade attributes. That is the starting point because the missing event concepts are where authority, owner, approval, precedence, effective period, source grade, adjusted grade, timestamp, and audit evidence would need to live for both systems to interpret the same rating value consistently.
  - causal path: The causal path starts with Exposure.risk_grade being derived from RiskRating.grade while also allowing report-team manual adjustment. The ontology also keeps multiple rating scales and leaves conversion to consumer systems, with mapping references outside the target artifact. Because rating attributes do not carry effective periods, assignment history, mapping versions, or override evidence, the model cannot determine which grade was authoritative at a given time, who changed it, why it changed, or whether the change was approved. That supports the issue that engine grades, reporting grades, and replayed historical grades can diverge without a common authority trail.
  - action: Fix this before the target ontology is considered complete by separating RatingAssignment, RatingMappingVersion or MappingApproval, and ManualRatingAdjustment or RatingOverride from current grade attributes. These concepts need owner/source authority, original and adjusted grade, approval authority/status, reason, effective period, timestamp, precedence, source system, supersession or versioning, and audit evidence so engine and reporting consumers use the same priority and replay rules.

- issue-005 (high): Issue-005 is a material shared-authority failure: the ontology keeps three rating scales but leaves conversion among them to consumer systems, so rating-scale changes can split engine and reporting behavior and make historical classifications non-replayable.
  - issue stance agreement: 4/6

  - raised by lenses: evolution
  - issue statement: Three rating scales are left to consumer-side conversion, so rating-scale evolution requires coordinated changes in every consuming system and can break continuity between engine and reports. Source finding context: credit-risk-ontology.yaml / classification_rules[0] and notes[0] Source finding context: Embedded Materialized Input: `classification_rules` states `Exposure.risk_grade는 RiskRating.grade에서 도출하되, 보고서 팀이 시장 상황에 따라 수동 조정할 수 있다.`; `notes` states `차주 등급(internal_rating 1~10, RiskRating R1~R5, Exposure risk_grade AAA~CCC)의 3개 스케일은 역사적 이유로 병존한다. 변환은 각 소비 시스템이 수행한다.` Source finding context: Because the ontology is intended as the shared concept authority for a risk engine and reporting system, leaving scale conversion outside the ontology means any new grade, rating-model change, or report override policy change must be reimplemented across consumers. Historical results also cannot be consistently reinterpreted because there is no versioned conversion authority tying `RiskRating.grade`, `Borrower.internal_rating`, and `Exposure.risk_grade` together over time. Source finding context: Add a canonical `RatingScale`/`RatingMapping` concept with source scale, target scale, mapping rules, owner, version, effective_from/effective_to, and override provenance. Make `Exposure.risk_grade` derivation reference that mapping rather than consumer-local conversion. Source finding context: .onto/review/20260716-acee24d9/round1/evolution.findings.yaml#evolution-candidate-001 Source finding context: The ontology's declared purpose as a shared concept authority for risk engine and reporting systems. Source finding context: When a rating scale changes, a new grade is introduced, or report-side manual adjustment policy changes, consumers must independently update conversion logic and may produce inconsistent or non-replayable classifications. Source finding context: This weakens review trust in the ontology as an extensible shared basis: the central classification concept is not change-tolerant, and engine/report continuity depends on undocumented external implementations. Source finding context: The ontology does not make rating conversion a versioned, owned concept inside the target artifact and instead delegates it to external consumer systems. Source finding context: The target contains three rating scales for borrower/internal, engine, and exposure/report classifications. Source finding context: The target does not define the conversion as an ontology entity or versioned relation; it points to an external wiki and each consumer system. Source finding context: Consumer-side conversion enables divergent behavior when grades or mapping rules evolve.

  - source lenses: evolution
  - action candidates: fix_before_release, fix_now
  - materiality: The declared purpose is to serve as the shared concept authority for risk engine and reporting systems. Because `Borrower.internal_rating`, `RiskRating.grade`, and `Exposure.risk_grade` coexist without an owned conversion authority, consumers must implement their own mappings. That weakens the ontology as the central basis for consistent classification, especially when grades, rating models, or report-side override policies change.
  - root cause: The causal chain starts at the missing ontology-owned rating conversion concept. The target identifies multiple scales and says conversion is performed by each consuming system or documented externally, but it does not define a versioned `RatingScale` or `RatingMapping` relation inside the artifact. Without that internal authority, downstream systems become the effective owners of mapping behavior.
  - causal path: The ontology contains separate borrower/internal, engine, and exposure/report rating scales. It then derives `Exposure.risk_grade` from `RiskRating.grade` while allowing report-team manual adjustment, but the mapping table and transformation rules live outside the ontology or in each consumer. As those scales or override policies evolve, each consumer can update differently, which creates divergent results and prevents consistent reinterpretation of historical ratings.
  - action: Add a canonical `RatingScale`/`RatingMapping` concept before release, including source scale, target scale, mapping rules, owner, version, effective_from/effective_to, and override provenance. `Exposure.risk_grade` derivation should reference that mapping rather than consumer-local conversion, so engine/report continuity and historical replay depend on a single versioned ontology authority.
  - unresolved disagreement: Deliberation resolved the root cause and final high issue severity, while preserving lens-specific disagreement: axiology and semantics lower severity to medium in their own frames, and logic does not find an independent contradiction beyond the authority/versioning concern.

- issue-008 (high): Collateral is materially mis-modeled because the ontology makes it both a subtype of Exposure and the asset that secures an Exposure. This collapses two different core concepts: the credit position being secured and the collateral asset/support that secures it.
  - issue stance agreement: 6/6
  - agreed or narrowed lenses: axiology, coverage, evolution, logic, semantics, structure
  - issue stance disagreement: 0/6
  - disagreeing stance lenses: none
  - not applicable lenses: none
  - insufficient evidence lenses: none
  - resolution accepted by: 4/4 deliberation participants
  - accepted lenses: evolution, logic, semantics, structure
  - remaining disagreement: 0/4 deliberation participants
  - remaining disagreement lenses: none
  - raised by lenses: evolution, logic, semantics
  - issue statement: Collateral is assigned incompatible roles as both an Exposure subtype and a securing asset related from Exposure, creating a type-level and lifecycle modeling conflict. Source finding context: credit-risk-ontology.yaml / Collateral entity inheritance Source finding context: Embedded Materialized Input: `Collateral.definition` says `익스포저를 담보하는 자산. 담보 평가의 기본 단위.` while `Collateral` also declares `is_a: Exposure`; relations include `{ from: Exposure, to: Collateral, kind: secured_by }`. Source finding context: Collateral is modeled both as a subtype of Exposure and as a secured asset related from Exposure, limiting future collateral model evolution. Source finding context: From an evolution perspective, this conflates credit exposure and securing asset lifecycles. Adding collateral-specific extensions such as pooled collateral, multiple valuations, guarantees as collateral support, lien priority, or asset substitution may require untangling inheritance because collateral would inherit exposure attributes like borrower reference, amount, product type, risk grade, and LTV even when those are not properties of the asset itself. Source finding context: Remove `Collateral is_a Exposure` and model collateral as a separate asset/support concept related to exposures through a relationship entity such as `SecurityAgreement` or `CollateralLink`, carrying allocation, priority, valuation, and effective dates. Source finding context: .onto/review/20260716-acee24d9/round1/evolution.findings.yaml#evolution-candidate-004 Source finding context: A change-tolerant concept basis for exposure and collateral management shared across systems. Source finding context: When collateral coverage expands beyond one asset directly attached to one exposure, the inherited exposure structure forces collateral records to carry exposure fields or requires a breaking remodel. Source finding context: The current inheritance choice narrows future collateral extensions and increases the likelihood of a disruptive schema change in a core risk-management concept. Source finding context: The ontology uses inheritance for an association/lifecycle relationship between exposure and collateral. Source finding context: Collateral is defined as an asset securing an exposure. Source finding context: The ontology simultaneously declares `Collateral is_a Exposure` and a separate `Exposure secured_by Collateral` relation. Source finding context: Inheritance makes collateral structurally depend on exposure attributes, constraining later collateral-specific lifecycle extensions. Source finding context: credit-risk-ontology.yaml / entities.Collateral Source finding context: Embedded Materialized Input: `Exposure` definition; `Collateral` definition with `is_a: Exposure`; relation `Exposure -> Collateral` with `kind: secured_by`. Source finding context: `Collateral` is simultaneously modeled as an `Exposure` subtype and as the asset secured by an `Exposure`, creating a type-level conflict for the same entity role. Source finding context: The conflict is formed by explicit claims in the artifact: `Exposure` is the credit exposure, `Collateral` is the securing asset, `Collateral is_a Exposure`, and the relation `Exposure secured_by Collateral` uses Collateral as the secured asset target. These cannot all be satisfied without collapsing exposure and collateral asset into the same class, which weakens the ontology as a shared concept authority for a risk engine and reporting system because systems may inherit exposure attributes onto collateral records or count collateral as exposure. Source finding context: Remove `is_a: Exposure` from `Collateral` and keep collateral connected through the `Exposure -> Collateral secured_by` relation; if a shared parent is needed, introduce a neutral parent such as `RiskObject` only with attributes valid for both concepts. Source finding context: .onto/review/20260716-acee24d9/round1/logic.findings.yaml#logic-candidate-001 Source finding context: Shared credit-risk concept authority for risk engine and reporting systems. Source finding context: When consumers interpret `is_a` as inheritance/subtyping, collateral assets become valid Exposure instances while also serving as assets securing exposures. Source finding context: This can make type-based consumers apply exposure fields, aggregation, or reporting logic to collateral records, undermining the declared shared taxonomy contract. Source finding context: The ontology uses `is_a` to connect Collateral to Exposure even though Collateral's own definition and relation role describe a secured asset, not a credit exposure. Source finding context: Collateral is declared as an asset that secures an exposure and as `is_a: Exposure`. Source finding context: Exposure is separately defined as the credit exposure to a borrower, and the relation declares Exposure as secured by Collateral. Source finding context: Together these claims require Collateral to be both a kind of Exposure and the asset target that secures an Exposure. Source finding context: credit-risk-ontology.yaml: Collateral entity Source finding context: .onto/review/20260716-acee24d9/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:39-46,85-88 Source finding context: Collateral is modeled as a subtype of Exposure even though its definition makes it a securing asset, not a credit exposure. Source finding context: A collateral asset and a credit exposure are different ontological kinds: one is the pledged asset, the other is the credit position being secured. Treating collateral as an Exposure weakens the shared concept standard because a risk engine or report can classify collateral records as exposure records or apply exposure attributes/aggregation semantics to assets. Source finding context: Remove `Collateral.is_a: Exposure`; keep Collateral as a separate asset entity connected through `Exposure secured_by Collateral`. If a shared superclass is needed, introduce a neutral parent such as `RiskObject` only after defining common semantics explicitly. Source finding context: .onto/review/20260716-acee24d9/round1/semantics.findings.yaml#semantics-candidate-001 Source finding context: A shared concept authority for risk engines and reporting systems covering exposure and collateral concepts. Source finding context: Any consumer that interprets `is_a` semantically will treat collateral assets as credit exposures despite the target also defining them as securing assets. Source finding context: This can corrupt classification, aggregation, and reporting semantics at the boundary between exposure records and collateral records. Source finding context: The subtype assertion uses `Exposure` as the parent for `Collateral` although the target definition assigns Collateral the meaning of a securing asset. Source finding context: Collateral is defined as an asset securing an exposure and the base unit of collateral appraisal. Source finding context: The same entity is declared `is_a: Exposure`. Source finding context: The relation section separately models Exposure as `secured_by` Collateral, confirming the intended distinction between exposure and collateral asset.

Source finding context: A change-tolerant concept basis for exposure and collateral management shared across systems. Source finding context: Shared credit-risk concept authority for risk engine and reporting systems.   - failure condition: A consumer interprets is_a as inheritance/subtyping, or collateral coverage expands to pooled collateral, multiple valuations, lien priority, guarantees, substitutions, or other collateral-specific lifecycles. Source finding context: When collateral coverage expands beyond one asset directly attached to one exposure, the inherited exposure structure forces collateral records to carry exposure fields or requires a breaking remodel. Source finding context: When consumers interpret `is_a` as inheritance/subtyping, collateral assets become valid Exposure instances while also serving as assets securing exposures. Source finding context: Any consumer that interprets `is_a` semantically will treat collateral assets as credit exposures despite the target also defining them as securing assets.
  - impact: Consumers may apply exposure fields, aggregation, or reporting logic to collateral records, corrupting the exposure/collateral boundary and forcing disruptive remodels for collateral evolution. Source finding context: The current inheritance choice narrows future collateral extensions and increases the likelihood of a disruptive schema change in a core risk-management concept. `Collateral` definition with `is_a: Exposure`; relation `Exposure -> Collateral` with `kind: secured_by`.`, `.onto/review/20260716-acee24d9/round1/semantics.findings.yaml#semantics-candidate-001`, `materialized-input.md:39-46`, `materialized-input.md:85-88`, `.onto/review/20260716-acee24d9/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:39-46,85-88`, `finding-ledger.yaml#finding-011`, `finding-ledger.yaml#finding-012`, `finding-ledger.yaml#finding-013`, `rel-012`, `finding-relation-graph.yaml#rel-012`, `rel-014`, `finding-relation-graph.yaml#rel-014`, `finding-011.cause-001`, `Embedded Materialized Input: `Collateral.definition`.`, `finding-011.cause-002`, `Embedded Materialized Input: `Collateral.is_a: Exposure`.`, `Embedded Materialized Input: relation from `Exposure` to `Collateral` kind `secured_by`.`, `finding-011.cause-003`, `Embedded Materialized Input: `Exposure` attributes include `borrower_ref`, `amount`, `product_type`, `risk_grade`, and `ltv`; `Collateral` has its own `collateral_id`, `collateral_type`, `appraised_value`, and `appraised_at`.`, `finding-012.cause-001`, `finding-012.cause-002`, `finding-012.cause-003`, `credit-risk-ontology.yaml: Exposure, Collateral, and relations sections in Embedded Materialized Input`, `finding-013.cause-001`, `materialized-input.md:39-40`, `finding-013.cause-002`, `materialized-input.md:41`, `finding-013.cause-003`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-008.axiology`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-008.coverage`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-008.evolution`, `issue-ledger.yaml#issue-008`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-008.logic`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-008.semantics`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-008.structure`, `issue-ledger.yaml#dep-004`, `rel-015`, `.onto/review/20260716-acee24d9/issue-ledger.yaml`, `.onto/review/20260716-acee24d9/problem-framing.yaml`
  - source lenses: evolution, logic, semantics
  - action candidates: fix_now
  - materiality: This weakens the declared shared concept authority for risk engines and reporting systems because consumers that honor `is_a` as subtype semantics can treat collateral assets as exposure records. That can cause exposure fields, aggregation, classification, and reporting behavior to be applied to collateral, corrupting the exposure/collateral boundary and making future collateral lifecycle extensions disruptive.
  - root cause: The root cause is the use of `Collateral is_a Exposure` while Collateral is also defined and related as a securing asset. That subtype assertion is the starting point because it gives Collateral the type and inherited structure of Exposure before any downstream consumer, report, or collateral extension can distinguish the asset/support role from the credit-exposure role.
  - causal path: The evidence path is consistent across the evolution, logic, and semantics findings: Collateral is defined as an asset securing an exposure; the ontology declares `Collateral is_a Exposure`; and the relation model separately states that Exposure is `secured_by` Collateral. Together, these claims require Collateral to be both a kind of Exposure and the target asset securing an Exposure, which creates a type-level conflict and propagates incorrect exposure semantics into collateral handling.
  - action: Remove `Collateral is_a Exposure` and model Collateral as a separate asset/support concept. Connect it to Exposure through a relationship entity such as `SecurityAgreement` or `CollateralLink` that can carry allocation, priority, valuation, and effective-date semantics, so collateral-specific lifecycle behavior can evolve without inheriting exposure attributes or breaking consumers.

- issue-002 (medium): 이 이슈는 차주 등급, 리스크 엔진 등급, 익스포저 등급의 병존 자체가 아니라, 그 사이의 변환 기준을 온톨로지의 canonical 개념으로 소유하지 않고 각 소비 시스템에 맡긴 점이 문제다. 따라서 공유 온톨로지는 등급 의미와 변환 기준의 단일 기준 역할을 하지 못한다.

Source finding context: credit-risk-ontology.yaml rating scale notes and RiskRating.grade Source finding context: .onto/review/20260716-acee24d9/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:20-22,53-56,97-98; .onto/review/20260716-acee24d9/execution-preparation/review-value-alignment-criteria.yaml:5-8 Source finding context: 이 온톨로지의 목적은 엔진과 보고 시스템이 공유할 개념 기준을 제공하는 것이다. 그런데 등급 변환의 의미와 책임을 각 소비 시스템으로 분산하면 같은 차주/익스포저 등급이 시스템별 변환표, 버전, 적용일에 따라 달라질 수 있다. 이는 역사적 병존이라는 국소 현실을 보존하는 대신 공유 기준이라는 상위 목적을 양보한 숨은 트레이드오프다. Source finding context: 세 등급 스케일을 인정하되, canonical RatingScale과 RatingMapping 개념을 온톨로지 안에 추가하라. source_scale, target_scale, source_grade, target_grade, mapping_version, owner, effective_from/effective_to, precedence를 정의하고 외부 위키는 비권위 참고가 아니라 해당 규칙의 출처/운영 절차로만 연결하라. Source finding context: .onto/review/20260716-acee24d9/round1/axiology.findings.yaml#axiology-candidate-002 Source finding context: 리스크 엔진과 보고 시스템 사이의 등급 의미 및 변환 기준 공유. Source finding context: 엔진, 보고서, 기타 소비 시스템이 각자 다른 변환표나 다른 시점의 위키 규칙으로 internal_rating/RiskRating/Exposure risk_grade를 변환하는 경우. Source finding context: 공유 온톨로지가 등급 의미의 단일 기준이 되지 못하고, 시스템별 해석 차이를 정당화하는 문서가 되어 보고 및 리스크 판단 간 불일치를 만든다. Source finding context: 등급 스케일 병존을 canonical mapping concept으로 승격하지 않고 소비 시스템별 변환 책임으로 남겼다. Source finding context: 차주 등급, 엔진 등급, 익스포저 등급의 세 스케일이 병존한다. Source finding context: 변환은 각 소비 시스템이 수행한다고 명시되어 있다. Source finding context: 온톨로지는 리스크 엔진과 보고 시스템이 공유하는 개념 권위 문서를 지향한다.
  - affected purpose: 리스크 엔진과 보고 시스템 사이의 등급 의미 및 변환 기준 공유.
  - failure condition: 엔진, 보고서, 기타 소비 시스템이 각자 다른 변환표나 다른 시점의 위키 규칙으로 internal_rating/RiskRating/Exposure risk_grade를 변환하는 경우.
  - impact: 공유 온톨로지가 등급 의미의 단일 기준이 되지 못하고, 시스템별 해석 차이를 정당화하는 문서가 되어 보고 및 리스크 판단 간 불일치를 만든다.
  - root hypothesis: 등급 스케일 병존을 canonical mapping concept으로 승격하지 않고 소비 시스템별 변환 책임으로 남겼다.
  - evidence: `.onto/review/20260716-acee24d9/round1/axiology.findings.yaml#axiology-candidate-002`, `materialized-input.md:20-22 defines Borrower.internal_rating 1-10`, `materialized-input.md:53-56 defines RiskRating.grade R1-R5 and external wiki mapping`, `materialized-input.md:97-98 states three scales coexist and each consumer performs conversion`, `review-value-alignment-criteria.yaml:5-8 states shared concept-standard review purpose`, `.onto/review/20260716-acee24d9/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:20-22,53-56,97-98; .onto/review/20260716-acee24d9/execution-preparation/review-value-alignment-criteria.yaml:5-8`, `finding-ledger.yaml#finding-002`, `finding-002.cause-001`, `materialized-input.md:20-22`, `materialized-input.md:53-56`, `materialized-input.md:97-98`, `finding-002.cause-002`, `finding-002.cause-003`, `materialized-input.md:6-8`, `review-value-alignment-criteria.yaml:5-8`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-002.axiology`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-002.coverage`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-002.evolution`, `issue-ledger.yaml#issue-002`, `.onto/review/20260716-acee24d9/round1/evolution.findings.yaml#evolution-candidate-001`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-002.logic`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-002.semantics`, `.onto/review/20260716-acee24d9/round1/semantics.findings.yaml#semantics-candidate-003`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-002.structure`, `.onto/review/20260716-acee24d9/issue-ledger.yaml`, `.onto/review/20260716-acee24d9/problem-framing.yaml`
  - source lenses: axiology
  - action candidates: fix_before_release, fix_now
  - materiality: 선언된 목적은 리스크 엔진과 보고 시스템이 등급 의미 및 변환 기준을 공유하도록 하는 것이다. 그런데 변환표, 버전, 적용일, 우선순위가 각 엔진·보고서·소비 시스템에 분산되면 같은 차주나 익스포저도 시스템마다 다르게 해석될 수 있다. 이는 공유 개념 기준이라는 목적을 약화시키며, 보고와 리스크 판단 간 불일치를 만들 수 있어 material issue로 유지된다.
  - root cause: 출발점은 세 등급 스케일의 존재가 아니라, 그 병존을 연결하는 RatingScale/RatingMapping 같은 canonical mapping concept이 온톨로지 안에 없다는 점이다. 변환 책임이 소비 시스템에 남아 있으면 변환 규칙의 소유자, 버전, 유효기간, 적용 우선순위가 공유 권위로 고정되지 않는다.
  - causal path: 근거 흐름은 세 단계다. 먼저 Borrower.internal_rating, RiskRating.grade, Exposure risk_grade처럼 서로 다른 등급 스케일이 병존한다. 다음으로 온톨로지는 이 변환을 각 소비 시스템이 수행한다고 둔다. 마지막으로 이 온톨로지는 엔진과 보고 시스템 사이의 공유 개념 권위 문서를 지향하므로, 변환 권위를 외부 소비자에게 분산하는 설계는 목적과 직접 충돌한다. Axiology, coverage, evolution, semantics 렌즈는 이 문제를 지지했고, logic 렌즈는 논리적 모순으로 볼 증거는 부족하다고 보았지만 이는 material root를 뒤집는 불일치로 남지 않았다.
  - action: 세 등급 스케일은 인정하되, 온톨로지 안에 canonical RatingScale과 RatingMapping을 추가해야 한다. RatingMapping에는 source_scale, target_scale, source_grade, target_grade, mapping_version, owner, effective_from/effective_to, precedence를 포함해 변환 권위와 적용 순서를 명시해야 한다. 외부 위키는 권위 있는 변환 기준 자체가 아니라 해당 규칙의 출처나 운영 절차로 연결해야 하며, 이 작업은 소비 시스템 구현보다 먼저 닫혀야 한다.

- issue-003 (medium): 한도 소진율과 RiskAppetite 준수 상태는 현재 공유된 시간 기준과 FX 권위 없이 월말 배치 ExposureAggregate 및 시스템별 환율표에 의존하므로, 같은 포트폴리오와 같은 판단 시점에서도 리스크 엔진과 보고 시스템이 서로 다른 utilization 또는 breached/compliant 상태를 산출할 수 있다.

Source finding context: credit-risk-ontology.yaml Limit utilization and ExposureAggregate temporality Source finding context: .onto/review/20260716-acee24d9/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:64-76,78-83,92-95; .onto/review/20260716-acee24d9/execution-preparation/review-value-alignment-criteria.yaml:5-8 Source finding context: RiskAppetite 준수 여부는 운영상 민감한 개념인데, 입력 총액은 월말 기준으로 낡을 수 있고 환산 기준은 시스템별로 달라질 수 있다. 공유 개념 권위 문서의 목적상 같은 시점의 같은 포트폴리오에 대해 엔진과 보고 시스템이 같은 준수 상태를 재현해야 하지만, 현재 설계는 시간 기준과 환율 권위를 분산시켜 서로 다른 소진율과 위반 여부를 허용한다. Source finding context: Limit 소진율과 RiskAppetite 준수 판단에 canonical valuation_time/as_of, aggregation_cutoff, freshness_status, fx_rate_source, fx_rate_timestamp, currency_basis를 추가하라. 월말 배치 값은 stale 가능성을 명시하고, 일중 판단이 필요한 준수 상태에는 별도 current exposure source 또는 'not current' 상태를 모델링하라. Source finding context: .onto/review/20260716-acee24d9/round1/axiology.findings.yaml#axiology-candidate-003 Source finding context: 리스크 엔진과 보고 시스템이 한도 소진율 및 RiskAppetite 준수 상태를 동일한 권위와 시간 기준으로 공유하는 것. Source finding context: 일중 신규 여신이 존재하거나 통화가 다른 한도/익스포저를 다룰 때, 시스템별 환율표와 월말 집계 시점 차이로 소진율 또는 breached/compliant 상태가 달라지는 경우. Source finding context: 위반 상태라는 핵심 운영 신호가 공유 온톨로지에서 재현 가능한 권위 값이 아니게 되어 보고 지연, 한도 초과 미탐지, 엔진-보고 불일치 위험을 만든다. Source finding context: 한도/준수 판단에 필요한 시간 기준과 환율 권위를 공유 개념으로 닫지 않고 소비 시스템과 배치 주기에 맡겼다. Source finding context: ExposureAggregate.total_amount는 월말 배치 저장 값이며 일중 신규 여신은 다음 배치까지 미반영된다. Source finding context: 통화가 다르면 각 시스템의 당일 환율 테이블로 Limit 소진율을 계산한다. Source finding context: RiskAppetite 준수 상태는 ExposureAggregate가 threshold를 초과하는지로 정의된다. Source finding context: 대상은 리스크 엔진과 보고서 시스템이 공유하는 개념 권위 문서를 지향하며, 검토 목적은 시간성·권위의 운영 위험을 포함한다.
  - affected purpose: 리스크 엔진과 보고 시스템이 한도 소진율 및 RiskAppetite 준수 상태를 동일한 권위와 시간 기준으로 공유하는 것.
  - failure condition: 일중 신규 여신이 존재하거나 통화가 다른 한도/익스포저를 다룰 때, 시스템별 환율표와 월말 집계 시점 차이로 소진율 또는 breached/compliant 상태가 달라지는 경우.
  - impact: 위반 상태라는 핵심 운영 신호가 공유 온톨로지에서 재현 가능한 권위 값이 아니게 되어 보고 지연, 한도 초과 미탐지, 엔진-보고 불일치 위험을 만든다.
  - root hypothesis: 한도/준수 판단에 필요한 시간 기준과 환율 권위를 공유 개념으로 닫지 않고 소비 시스템과 배치 주기에 맡겼다.
  - evidence: `.onto/review/20260716-acee24d9/round1/axiology.findings.yaml#axiology-candidate-003`, `materialized-input.md:69-76 defines monthly-batch ExposureAggregate and intraday omission`, `materialized-input.md:78-83 defines RiskAppetite compliance_status from ExposureAggregate threshold breach`, `materialized-input.md:92-95 defines utilization and system-specific FX tables`, `review-value-alignment-criteria.yaml:5-8 highlights shared concept standard and temporality concerns`, `.onto/review/20260716-acee24d9/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:64-76,78-83,92-95; .onto/review/20260716-acee24d9/execution-preparation/review-value-alignment-criteria.yaml:5-8`, `finding-ledger.yaml#finding-003`, `finding-003.cause-001`, `materialized-input.md:69-76`, `finding-003.cause-002`, `materialized-input.md:92-95`, `finding-003.cause-003`, `materialized-input.md:78-83`, `finding-003.cause-004`, `materialized-input.md:6-8`, `review-value-alignment-criteria.yaml:5-8`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-003.axiology`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-003.coverage`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-003.evolution`, `issue-ledger.yaml#issue-003`, `.onto/review/20260716-acee24d9/round1/evolution.findings.yaml#evolution-candidate-002`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-003.logic`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-003.semantics`, `.onto/review/20260716-acee24d9/round1/semantics.findings.yaml#semantics-candidate-004`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-003.structure`, `issue-ledger.yaml#dep-002`, `rel-008`, `issue-ledger.yaml#dep-003`, `rel-011`, `.onto/review/20260716-acee24d9/issue-ledger.yaml`, `.onto/review/20260716-acee24d9/problem-framing.yaml`
  - source lenses: axiology
  - action candidates: fix_before_release, fix_now
  - materiality: 이 문제는 리스크 엔진과 보고 시스템이 한도 소진율 및 RiskAppetite 준수 상태를 동일한 권위와 시간 기준으로 공유해야 한다는 목적을 약화한다. 준수 상태는 운영상 핵심 신호인데, 일중 신규 여신이 월말 배치 전까지 반영되지 않거나 통화 환산 기준이 시스템별로 달라지면 보고 지연, 한도 초과 미탐지, 엔진-보고 불일치가 발생할 수 있다.
  - root cause: 출발점은 한도/준수 판단에 필요한 시간 기준과 환율 권위를 공유 개념으로 닫지 않고 소비 시스템과 배치 주기에 맡긴 것이다. 이 때문에 valuation_time/as_of, aggregation_cutoff, freshness_status, fx_rate_source, fx_rate_timestamp, currency_basis가 재현 가능한 권위 값으로 고정되지 않는다.
  - causal path: ExposureAggregate.total_amount는 월말 배치 저장 값이라 일중 신규 여신을 다음 배치까지 누락할 수 있고, 통화가 다르면 각 시스템의 당일 환율 테이블이 Limit 소진율 계산에 쓰인다. RiskAppetite 준수 상태는 이 ExposureAggregate가 threshold를 초과하는지로 정의되므로, 낡은 집계 값과 분산된 FX 기준이 그대로 breached/compliant 판단 차이로 이어진다.
  - action: Limit 소진율과 RiskAppetite 준수 판단에 canonical valuation_time/as_of, aggregation_cutoff, freshness_status, fx_rate_source, fx_rate_timestamp, currency_basis를 모델링해야 한다. 월말 배치 값은 stale 가능성을 명시하고, 일중 준수 판단이 필요한 경우 별도 current exposure source 또는 'not current' 상태를 두어 현재성 없는 값을 현재 준수 상태처럼 소비하지 못하게 해야 한다.

- issue-004 (medium): Default and delinquency states are materially missing from the shared credit-risk ontology as a minimal external-authority lifecycle-status reference, so the ontology cannot provide a common basis for post-transition exposure classification across risk engines and reporting.
  - issue stance agreement: 3/6
  - agreed or narrowed lenses: coverage, axiology, evolution

  - issue statement: The ontology leaves delinquency/default lifecycle states outside the shared credit-risk concept standard even though those states affect risk classification, reporting, limits, and collateral treatment. Source finding context: credit-risk-ontology.yaml / Exposure and notes[1] Source finding context: Embedded materialized input: Exposure entity; notes[1] Source finding context: 연체·부실 상태를 범위 밖으로 제외해 여신 리스크 분류의 상태 lifecycle이 정상 익스포저 중심으로만 닫히고, 부실 전이 이후의 리스크 상태 범주가 누락된다. Source finding context: 대상은 여신 리스크 관리의 개념 모델로서 익스포저, 등급, 한도, 담보를 정의하고 리스크 엔진과 보고 시스템이 공유할 기준을 지향한다. 그러나 여신 리스크에서 익스포저 lifecycle의 핵심 상태인 연체·부실을 제외하면, 리스크 엔진 등급과 보고 시스템의 상태 기반 집계가 같은 차주/익스포저를 어느 단계로 분류해야 하는지 공유할 수 없다. 별도 시스템이 소유하더라도 최소한 외부 권위 상태 참조, 기준시점, 수신 이벤트 개념은 필요하다. Source finding context: DefaultStatus 또는 ExposureLifecycleStatus를 온톨로지에 최소 참조 개념으로 포함하고, source_system, status, status_effective_at, status_received_at, status_history_ref를 둔다. 연체관리 시스템이 권위 원본이라면 out-of-scope 배제가 아니라 외부 권위 참조로 명시해 보고와 엔진이 동일 상태를 사용할 수 있게 한다. Source finding context: .onto/review/20260716-acee24d9/round1/coverage.findings.yaml#coverage-candidate-004 Source finding context: 여신 리스크 분류 온톨로지의 주요 리스크 상태 포괄성과 리스크 엔진·보고 시스템 간 상태 기준 공유 Source finding context: 익스포저가 연체 또는 부실로 전이되어 등급, 한도 소진, 담보 평가, 보고 집계가 상태에 따라 달라져야 하는 경우 Source finding context: 온톨로지가 정상/등급/한도 중심 상태만 제공하고 부실 상태 참조를 배제하므로 주요 리스크 lifecycle 구간이 공유 기준 밖에 남아 보고·엔진 간 분류 불일치가 발생할 수 있다. Source finding context: 외부 시스템 소유 상태를 공유 온톨로지 안의 참조 가능한 lifecycle 개념으로 모델링하지 않고 범위 밖으로 배제한 범위 결정 Source finding context: 대상은 여신 리스크 관리 개념 모델로 Exposure를 핵심 엔티티로 둔다. Source finding context: Exposure에는 상태나 상태 이력 속성이 없다. Source finding context: 연체/부실(default) 상태는 별도 시스템에서 다룬다는 이유로 온톨로지 범위 밖으로 제외된다. Source finding context: 그 결과 익스포저 lifecycle의 부실 전이 이후 구간이 공유 개념 기준에 포함되지 않는다.
  - affected purpose: Completeness of the shared exposure-risk lifecycle basis for risk-engine and reporting classification. Source finding context: 여신 리스크 분류 온톨로지의 주요 리스크 상태 포괄성과 리스크 엔진·보고 시스템 간 상태 기준 공유
  - failure condition: An exposure transitions into delinquent or default state and downstream grades, aggregations, collateral handling, or reports depend on that state. Source finding context: 익스포저가 연체 또는 부실로 전이되어 등급, 한도 소진, 담보 평가, 보고 집계가 상태에 따라 달라져야 하는 경우
  - impact: A core risk lifecycle segment remains outside the shared standard, allowing engine and reporting consumers to classify the same exposure differently after default transition. Source finding context: 온톨로지가 정상/등급/한도 중심 상태만 제공하고 부실 상태 참조를 배제하므로 주요 리스크 lifecycle 구간이 공유 기준 밖에 남아 보고·엔진 간 분류 불일치가 발생할 수 있다.
  - root hypothesis: External default/delinquency state is excluded from the shared ontology instead of modeled as a minimal authoritative lifecycle-status reference with source and timing metadata.
  - evidence: `.onto/review/20260716-acee24d9/round1/coverage.findings.yaml#coverage-candidate-004`, `Exposure.definition: 차주에 대한 신용 익스포저(대출, 한도성 여신 포함).`, `Exposure.attributes: exposure_id, borrower_ref, amount, product_type, risk_grade, ltv`, `notes[1]: 연체/부실(default) 상태는 별도 시스템(연체관리)에서 다루므로 이 온톨로지의 범위 밖이다.`, `Embedded materialized input: Exposure entity; notes[1]`, `finding-ledger.yaml#finding-007`, `finding-007.cause-001`, `header comment in embedded materialized input`, `Exposure.definition`, `finding-007.cause-002`, `Exposure.attributes`, `finding-007.cause-003`, `notes[1]`, `finding-007.cause-004`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-004.axiology`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-004.coverage`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-004.evolution`, `issue-ledger.yaml#issue-004`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-004.logic`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-004.semantics`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-004.structure`, `Authoritative delinquency/default status vocabulary and transition semantics if a richer lifecycle model is later proposed.`, `.onto/review/20260716-acee24d9/issue-ledger.yaml`, `.onto/review/20260716-acee24d9/problem-framing.yaml`
  - source lenses: coverage
  - action candidates: fix_before_release, fix_now
  - materiality: This weakens completeness of the shared exposure-risk lifecycle basis because Exposure is a core entity, but its modeled attributes stop at identifiers, borrower, amount, product type, risk grade, and LTV while default/delinquency status is explicitly excluded. When an exposure becomes delinquent or defaults, grades, reporting aggregation, limits, collateral treatment, and risk classification may depend on that state; without a shared reference, consumers can classify the same exposure differently.
  - root cause: The starting point is the scope decision to leave externally owned default/delinquency state outside the ontology instead of representing it as a minimal authoritative reference. The issue is not that this ontology must own the full default-management lifecycle, but that it lacks a sourced, time-stamped status reference to the external authority.
  - causal path: The causal chain runs from Exposure being a central concept, to Exposure lacking status or status-history attributes, to the note that default/delinquency is handled by a separate delinquency-management system and therefore excluded. That exclusion leaves the post-delinquency/default segment of the exposure lifecycle outside the shared concept standard, which creates the classification and reporting consistency gap.
  - action: Add a minimal DefaultStatus or ExposureLifecycleStatus reference concept linked to the external authority, including source_system, status, status_effective_at, status_received_at, and status_history_ref. This should preserve the external system as the authority while giving risk-engine and reporting consumers the same lifecycle status basis; richer status vocabulary and transition semantics can be defined later once authoritative evidence is available.

- issue-006 (medium): Issue-006 is a material utilization-calculation provenance and authority gap: limit utilization is defined from stored exposure aggregates, approved limits, and FX conversion, but the ontology lacks canonical snapshot, version, source, cutoff/as-of, and valuation metadata needed to replay the calculation consistently.
  - issue stance agreement: 3/6
  - agreed or narrowed lenses: axiology, coverage, evolution
  - issue stance disagreement: 0/6
  - disagreeing stance lenses: none
  - not applicable lenses: none
  - insufficient evidence lenses: logic, semantics, structure

  - accepted lenses: evolution, coverage, axiology, logic, semantics, structure
  - remaining disagreement: 0/6 deliberation participants
  - remaining disagreement lenses: none
  - raised by lenses: evolution, coverage
  - issue statement: Shared-root issue across finding-009, finding-006: The ontology combines stored exposure aggregates, approved limits, and FX conversion without canonical snapshot/version/source metadata for replayable utilization calculations. Source finding context: credit-risk-ontology.yaml / ExposureAggregate and classification_rules[1] Source finding context: Embedded Materialized Input: `ExposureAggregate.total_amount` note says `월말 배치로 계산해 저장. 일중 신규 여신은 다음 배치까지 미반영.`; classification rule says `Limit 소진율 = ExposureAggregate.total_amount / Limit.approved_amount. 통화가 다른 경우 환산은 각 시스템의 당일 환율 테이블을 사용한다.` Source finding context: Exposure aggregation and limit-utilization rules are not temporally versioned, so adding intraday products, currencies, or reporting cutoffs can break continuity of limit calculations. Source finding context: A credit risk taxonomy that must serve both engines and reports needs stable temporal semantics for replay, audit, and future product/currency expansion. Here, intraday exposures are explicitly excluded until the next batch, while limit utilization uses that stale aggregate and system-local FX. When new intraday facilities, currencies, or reporting frequencies are added, the existing concepts cannot absorb them without changing calculation semantics or accepting inconsistent utilization outputs. Source finding context: Introduce explicit temporal and valuation concepts: `ExposureSnapshot` or `ExposureAggregate` with calculation_frequency, included_exposure_cutoff, as_of timestamp, source batch/run id, and FX source/rate_time. Define limit utilization against a named snapshot and canonical FX policy. Source finding context: .onto/review/20260716-acee24d9/round1/evolution.findings.yaml#evolution-candidate-002 Source finding context: Shared risk-engine and reporting basis for limit utilization and exposure aggregation. Source finding context: When the ontology must support intraday lending, additional currencies, or different report cutoffs, the same borrower and limit can receive different utilization results depending on batch timing and each system's FX table. Source finding context: The ontology is not evolution-resilient for temporal expansion: future reporting or engine changes require reworking core aggregate semantics and may disrupt comparability with historical stored totals. Source finding context: The ontology combines stored batch aggregates with system-local FX conversion but lacks canonical snapshot/version and valuation-time concepts. Source finding context: `ExposureAggregate.total_amount` is defined as the total of all exposure amounts but is stored from a month-end batch that omits intraday new credit until the next batch. Source finding context: Limit utilization is computed from that aggregate and approved limit amount. Source finding context: Currency conversion is delegated to each system's same-day FX table, leaving no stable valuation authority in the ontology. Source finding context: Future intraday, multi-currency, or higher-frequency reporting changes can alter utilization results without a versioned snapshot or FX policy preserving continuity. Source finding context: credit-risk-ontology.yaml / Limit.approved_amount and classification_rules[1] Source finding context: Embedded materialized input: Limit.attributes.approved_amount note; classification_rules[1] Source finding context: 한도와 환율처럼 복수 시스템에 걸쳐 관리되는 값에 단일 원본 권위와 기준시점 개념이 누락되어 한도 소진율 계산 기준이 분산된다. Source finding context: Limit 소진율은 ExposureAggregate.total_amount / Limit.approved_amount로 정의되어 리스크 위반 판단과 보고에 직접 쓰일 수 있는 지표다. 그런데 구성 값의 단일 권위와 환산 기준이 없으면 리스크 엔진과 보고 시스템이 같은 차주·같은 날짜에 서로 다른 소진율을 계산할 수 있으며, 공유 개념 기준으로서의 역할이 약해진다. Source finding context: LimitSourceAuthority 또는 BookOfRecord 개념을 추가해 승인 시스템, CRM, 환율 테이블의 역할과 우선순위를 명시한다. CurrencyConversionRate에는 rate_source, rate_timestamp, effective_date, currency_pair, version을 두고, LimitUtilization 계산에는 사용한 원천값과 환율 기준을 참조하게 한다. Source finding context: .onto/review/20260716-acee24d9/round1/coverage.findings.yaml#coverage-candidate-003 Source finding context: 리스크 엔진과 보고 시스템이 공유하는 한도 소진율 및 통화 환산 기준 Source finding context: 승인 시스템과 CRM의 한도 값이 다르거나 각 시스템의 당일 환율 테이블이 다른 값을 제공하는 경우 Source finding context: 소진율 계산의 입력 권위와 환산 기준이 분산되어 동일 익스포저에 대해 시스템별 결과가 달라질 수 있고, RiskAppetite 위반 판단 또는 보고 숫자의 재현성이 떨어진다. `.onto/review/20260716-acee24d9/issue-ledger.yaml`, `.onto/review/20260716-acee24d9/problem-framing.yaml`
  - source lenses: evolution, coverage
  - action candidates: fix_before_release, fix_now
  - materiality: This weakens the declared shared risk-engine and reporting purpose because the same borrower and limit can produce different utilization results when batch timing, report cutoffs, limit sources, or system-local FX tables differ. Since utilization can drive risk appetite breach checks and reporting, missing shared authority over inputs and valuation timing undermines reproducibility and comparability.
  - root cause: The root cause starts at the ontology boundary: it models the aggregate amount, approved limit, and currency conversion result path without naming the authoritative snapshot, source of record, run/version, cutoff, or FX valuation policy. Because these authorities are absent upstream, downstream engines and reports have to infer or choose them locally.
  - causal path: Finding-009 shows that ExposureAggregate.total_amount is a stored month-end batch value, omits intraday credit until the next batch, and has only date-level as_of context; finding-006 shows that approved limits and FX inputs are distributed across approval/CRM and each system's same-day FX table. Limit utilization then divides the stored aggregate by the approved limit, with currency conversion delegated per system. That path makes inconsistent or non-replayable utilization a direct consequence of missing snapshot and valuation authority, especially as intraday products, more currencies, or different cutoffs are added.
  - action: Fix this before release by making utilization depend on explicit temporal and valuation concepts: define ExposureSnapshot or extend ExposureAggregate with calculation_frequency, included_exposure_cutoff, as_of timestamp, source batch/run id, and version; add source-of-record or BookOfRecord/LimitSourceAuthority concepts for limit values; add CurrencyConversionRate metadata such as rate_source, rate_timestamp, effective_date, currency_pair, and version. LimitUtilization should reference the named snapshot, authoritative limit source, and canonical FX policy so replay and reporting use the same inputs.
  - unresolved disagreement: Deliberation narrowed the issue to a provenance and authority gap primarily supported by evolution and coverage with axiology materiality support; it should not be restated as a fully supported formal logic, semantics-led, or graph-structure defect without additional lens-specific evidence.

- issue-007 (medium): Issue-007 remains a material evolvability problem: core credit-risk classifications are modeled as closed inline enums, so ordinary growth in segments, products, collateral classes, or grades requires schema and validator edits instead of additive taxonomy management.
  - issue stance agreement: 3/6
  - agreed or narrowed lenses: coverage, evolution, axiology
  - issue stance disagreement: 0/6
  - disagreeing stance lenses: none
  - not applicable lenses: logic, structure
  - insufficient evidence lenses: semantics
  - resolution accepted by: 0/0 deliberation participants
  - accepted lenses: none
  - remaining disagreement: 0/0 deliberation participants
  - remaining disagreement lenses: none
  - raised by lenses: evolution
  - issue statement: The ontology makes core categories such as segment, product type, collateral type, and grades brittle under normal portfolio, regulatory, or reporting evolution. Source finding context: credit-risk-ontology.yaml / enumerated category attributes Source finding context: Embedded Materialized Input: `Borrower.segment` values `[retail, sme, corporate]`; `Exposure.product_type` values `[term_loan, revolving, guarantee]`; `Collateral.collateral_type` values `[real_estate, deposit, securities]`; `Exposure.risk_grade` and `RiskRating.grade` are also closed enum lists. Source finding context: Core business classifications are modeled as closed inline enums, so new borrower segments, products, collateral classes, or grades require schema edits rather than additive extension. Source finding context: For an evolving credit-risk concept standard, categories such as product type, collateral type, segment, and grade are expected to expand as portfolios, regulations, and reporting needs change. Inline closed enums force changes directly into existing attribute definitions, increasing migration pressure and making it harder to preserve historical category meaning or support consumer compatibility. Source finding context: Promote volatile classifications to managed taxonomy concepts such as `ProductType`, `CollateralType`, `BorrowerSegment`, and `GradeCode` with stable ids, labels, active/deprecated status, version/effective dates, and parent/category relations. Keep attributes as references to those taxonomy entries. Source finding context: .onto/review/20260716-acee24d9/round1/evolution.findings.yaml#evolution-candidate-003 Source finding context: Extensible shared classification ontology for credit-risk engine and reporting concepts. Source finding context: When a new segment, product, collateral class, or grade is introduced, the ontology must edit existing enum declarations and downstream validators instead of adding a new taxonomy record with compatibility metadata. Source finding context: This creates brittle evolution and migration risk in the core classification surface, especially for systems that rely on the ontology as a shared validation and reporting basis. Source finding context: The ontology encodes volatile classification vocabularies as closed attribute-local enums instead of first-class extensible taxonomy concepts. Source finding context: Multiple core classification axes are closed inline enum lists inside attributes. Source finding context: Inline enum expansion requires modifying existing attribute schemas rather than adding separate taxonomy records. Source finding context: Without lifecycle or effective-version metadata, future category changes cannot preserve historical meaning or compatibility in the ontology itself.
  - affected purpose: Extensible shared classification ontology for credit-risk engine and reporting concepts.
  - failure condition: A new borrower segment, product type, collateral class, or grade must be introduced while preserving historical meaning and downstream compatibility. Source finding context: When a new segment, product, collateral class, or grade is introduced, the ontology must edit existing enum declarations and downstream validators instead of adding a new taxonomy record with compatibility metadata.
  - impact: Schema edits and consumer validator changes become necessary for ordinary category growth, increasing migration pressure and weakening continuity of the shared classification surface. Source finding context: This creates brittle evolution and migration risk in the core classification surface, especially for systems that rely on the ontology as a shared validation and reporting basis.
  - root hypothesis: Volatile business classifications are encoded as closed attribute-local enums rather than managed taxonomy concepts with lifecycle and compatibility metadata.
  - evidence: `.onto/review/20260716-acee24d9/round1/evolution.findings.yaml#evolution-candidate-003`, `Embedded Materialized Input: `Borrower.segment` inline enum values.`, `Embedded Materialized Input: `Exposure.product_type` inline enum values.`, `Embedded Materialized Input: `Collateral.collateral_type` inline enum values.`, `Embedded Materialized Input: `RiskRating.grade` and `Exposure.risk_grade` inline enum values.`, `Embedded Materialized Input: `Borrower.segment` values `[retail, sme, corporate]`; `Exposure.product_type` values `[term_loan, revolving, guarantee]`; `Collateral.collateral_type` values `[real_estate, deposit, securities]`; `Exposure.risk_grade` and `RiskRating.grade` are also closed enum lists.`, `finding-ledger.yaml#finding-010`, `finding-010.cause-001`, `Embedded Materialized Input: `Borrower.segment`, `Exposure.product_type`, `Collateral.collateral_type`, `RiskRating.grade`, and `Exposure.risk_grade` enum declarations.`, `finding-010.cause-002`, `Embedded Materialized Input: enum values are embedded directly under each attribute; no separate taxonomy entities for product, collateral, segment, or grade codes are defined.`, `finding-010.cause-003`, `Embedded Materialized Input: no enum entries include active/deprecated status, aliases, or effective dates; ontology version is only global `1.1`.`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-007.axiology`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-007.coverage`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-007.evolution`, `issue-ledger.yaml#issue-007`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-007.logic`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-007.semantics`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-007.structure`, `.onto/review/20260716-acee24d9/issue-ledger.yaml`, `.onto/review/20260716-acee24d9/problem-framing.yaml`
  - source lenses: evolution
  - action candidates: follow_up
  - materiality: This weakens the declared purpose of an extensible shared classification ontology because the categories most likely to change are embedded directly in attribute definitions. When portfolios, reporting needs, or regulatory expectations add a category, consumers must absorb schema changes rather than reference a stable classification record with compatibility metadata.
  - root cause: The starting point is that volatile business classifications are encoded as attribute-local closed enums rather than managed taxonomy concepts. Because the enum values themselves are the classification authority, there is no separate place to attach stable ids, labels, aliases, active or deprecated status, effective dates, or parent/category relationships.
  - causal path: The causal path is direct: borrower segment, exposure product type, collateral type, and risk grades are closed enum lists inside attributes; expanding any of them means changing those attribute schemas rather than adding taxonomy records; without lifecycle and effective-version metadata, the ontology cannot preserve historical category meaning or provide compatibility guidance to downstream validators and reports.
  - action: Promote these volatile classifications to managed taxonomy concepts such as ProductType, CollateralType, BorrowerSegment, and GradeCode, with stable ids, labels, aliases, status, effective/version dates, and parent/category relations. Then keep the entity attributes as references to those taxonomy entries so category growth is additive and downstream compatibility can be managed explicitly.

- issue-009 (medium): Issue-009 is material: Exposure.ltv is presented as a collateralized risk ratio, but the ontology does not define whether that value is calculated, manually entered, snapshotted, or overridden. That leaves consumers unable to know which LTV value is authoritative for risk rules or reporting.
  - issue stance agreement: 4/6
  - agreed or narrowed lenses: axiology, coverage, semantics, evolution
  - issue stance disagreement: 0/6
  - disagreeing stance lenses: none
